# Notification matrix

All wording below is proposed English/French/Arabic copy pending qualified language/domain review. Each transition row resolves to the actual complete subject/body in M01–M10; placeholders are runtime references, not omitted authoring work. The same safe wording is intentionally reused for different transitions; the secure record shows the exact state/action in the reviewed interface language.

## Delivery rules

C1 = in-app, stored only for eligible account recipients. C2 = explicitly manual office handoff/call/print with actual recorder/time. C3 = optional configured external channel with actual recipient verification/provider outcome; currently not operationally verified. No C3 row may claim sent/delivered until connected and evidenced. A scheduled configuration without runner is a manual worklist, not sent. Guest notifications use C2 unless a real C3 route is approved; private tracking displays current permitted state when visited.

Operational own-case/security/access notices use mandatory in-app availability, separate from optional social/community preferences. Optional C3/community updates honor channel preferences, proposed quiet hours 21:00–08:00 parish time and optional daily digest at 08:00; these are unapproved defaults and require a real runner. No emergency dispatch is implied. Replacing preferences does not erase a saved notice.

The sender is the actual parish/office/service identity plus internal acting account where applicable. Reviewer/incident/pastoral notices contain no identity/category/case title outside their secure audience. M05 contains no reference in the external subject/body. Tokens, medical data, donor amounts, allegations, instruction notes and other people’s names are forbidden in notification previews.

Deduplicate by transition operation + recipient account + channel + template version. A person holding several roles receives one notice per relevant event/channel. Retry only failed attempts after rechecking recipient scope/contact; never resend already delivered messages on page load. Record Saved in-app, Manual handoff recorded, Provider accepted, Delivered if evidenced, Read if explicitly recorded, Failed and Cancelled separately. Proposed retries: immediate, 15 minutes, 2 hours; then assigned delivery-owner worklist. No automated retries until a worker exists.

| Message | EN subject / body | FR sujet / texte | AR الموضوع / النص |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| M01 | Record update — There is an update to {reference}. Open the secure record for details and your next step. | Mise à jour du dossier — Une mise à jour est disponible pour {reference}. Ouvrez le dossier sécurisé pour consulter les détails et la prochaine étape. | تحديث السجل — يتوفر تحديث للمرجع {reference}. افتح السجل الآمن للاطلاع على التفاصيل والخطوة التالية. |
| M02 | Information needed — Please open {reference} to review the requested information and reply securely. | Informations nécessaires — Ouvrez {reference} pour consulter les informations demandées et répondre de manière sécurisée. | معلومات مطلوبة — افتح المرجع {reference} للاطلاع على المعلومات المطلوبة والرد بأمان. |
| M03 | Action recorded for your review — An action attributed to your instruction was recorded on {reference}. Review its details; no repeat approval is requested. | Action enregistrée selon votre instruction — Une action fondée sur votre instruction a été enregistrée dans {reference}. Consultez les détails ; aucune nouvelle approbation ne vous est demandée. | تسجيل إجراء بناءً على تعليماتك — سُجّل إجراء بناءً على تعليماتك في المرجع {reference}. راجع التفاصيل؛ لا يُطلب منك اعتماد القرار مرة أخرى. |
| M04 | Processing update — Your request {reference} is being processed. Open it to see whether any action is required from you. | Suivi du traitement — Votre demande {reference} est en cours de traitement. Ouvrez-la pour savoir si une action est attendue de votre part. | متابعة الطلب — طلبك ذو المرجع {reference} قيد المعالجة. افتحه لمعرفة ما إذا كان يلزمك اتخاذ إجراء. |
| M05 | Confidential case update — There is an update to your confidential case. Sign in to view it privately. | Mise à jour confidentielle — Une mise à jour de votre dossier confidentiel est disponible. Connectez-vous pour la consulter en privé. | تحديث سري — يتوفر تحديث لملفك السري. سجّل الدخول للاطلاع عليه بصورة خاصة. |
| M06 | Review your account access — Your account access has changed. Sign in to review your own current assignments and available actions. | Vérifiez vos accès — Vos accès ont changé. Connectez-vous pour consulter vos affectations actuelles et les actions disponibles. | راجع صلاحيات حسابك — تغيّرت صلاحيات حسابك. سجّل الدخول لمراجعة تكليفاتك الحالية والإجراءات المتاحة. |
| M07 | Change to a planned activity — A planned activity linked to {reference} has changed. Review the new details and any confirmation or refund options. | Modification d’une activité prévue — Une activité liée à {reference} a changé. Consultez les nouvelles informations et les éventuelles options de confirmation ou de remboursement. | تغيير في نشاط مقرر — تغيّر نشاط مرتبط بالمرجع {reference}. راجع التفاصيل الجديدة وخيارات التأكيد أو استرداد المبلغ، إن وُجدت. |
| M08 | Delivery needs attention — The record {reference} was saved, but a secondary delivery or attachment step failed. Open it to retry only the failed step. | Une étape nécessite votre attention — Le dossier {reference} a été enregistré, mais une étape d’envoi ou de pièce jointe a échoué. Ouvrez-le pour réessayer uniquement cette étape. | خطوة تتطلب المتابعة — حُفظ السجل ذو المرجع {reference}، لكن تعذرت خطوة إرسال أو إرفاق. افتحه لإعادة محاولة الخطوة المتعذرة فقط. |
| M09 | Invitation available — An invitation is available. Review the named organization, purpose and expiry before accepting through the private link provided by the authorized office. | Invitation disponible — Une invitation est disponible. Vérifiez l’organisation, l’objet et la date d’expiration avant de l’accepter au moyen du lien privé remis par le bureau autorisé. | دعوة متاحة — تتوفر دعوة. راجع الجهة والغرض وتاريخ الانتهاء قبل قبولها عبر الرابط الخاص الذي يقدمه المكتب المخوّل. |
| M10 | Deadline approaching — A deadline for {reference} is approaching. Open the record to see the actual date and the permitted next action. | Échéance proche — Une échéance pour {reference} approche. Ouvrez le dossier pour consulter la date exacte et l’action autorisée. | اقتراب الموعد النهائي — يقترب موعد نهائي للمرجع {reference}. افتح السجل لمعرفة التاريخ الفعلي والإجراء المسموح به. |

## Timed and amendment triggers

| Trigger | Recipients/template | Timing/channel/stop | Privacy and actual capability |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Appointment expiry | Appointee + appointing authority / M10 | 30 and 7 days before end; shorter than 7 days: once at halfway point; C1/C2 manual until runner; stop on revoke/renew/end | Only own role/scope/end date inside secure record |
| 24-hour room hold expiry | Resource owner/requester / M10 then M01 | At stated end; evaluate expiry at next availability/protected request even without runner; do not promise push at exact minute | No competing applicant details |
| 48-hour waitlist offer | Invited applicant + event lead / M10/M01 | Reminder at 24h when still pending; expiry action releases offer and reviews next eligible applicant; short-event window explicitly stated | No other waitlisted families |
| Material date/room/transport/consent amendment | Affected confirmed participants/verified guardians, resource/clergy/volunteer owners / M07 | Immediately after approved amendment; reacknowledgment deadline shown; actual old/new details in secure case | Do not treat delivery as acknowledgment or consent |
| No response to office information request | Current office owner / M10; applicant only through permitted channel | Proposed internal check after 3 working days, priest escalation after 5; applicant-facing SLA only if parish commits | Do not auto-decline, erase evidence or auto-charge |
| Confidential reviewer recusal/revocation | Independent routing authority/new eligible selected reviewers / M05 | Immediate in-app access change; manual independent handover if no runner | No notice/count to broad office or implicated authority |
